muddhaṃ muddhādhipātañca        taṃ suṇoma vaco tava .

Ahampetaṃ [^525-3]  na jānāmi     ñāṇamettha [^525-4] na vijjati    
muddhaṃ muddhādhipāto ca       jinānaṃ heta [^525-5] dassanaṃ .

Atha ko carahi jānāti       asmiṃ paṭhavimaṇḍale    
muddhaṃ muddhādhipātañca        taṃ me akkhāhi devate .

Purā kapilavatthumhā         nikkhanto lokanāyako    
apacco okkākarājassa     sakyaputto pabhaṅkaro .

So hi brāhmaṇa sambuddho    sabbadhammāna pāragū    
sabbābhiññābalappatto       sabbadhammesu cakkhumā    
sabbakammakkhayaṃ patto        vimutto upadhikkhaye [^525-6]    
buddho so bhagavā loke     dhammaṃ desesi cakkhumā    
#[^525-1] po. Cintentassa .  [^525-2] po. Muddhaṃ muddhādhipāte vā .  [^525-3] po. Ahametaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 526 -->
<a id="page-526"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 526]*

Taṃ tvaṃ gantvāna pucchassu     so te taṃ byākarissati .

Sambuddhoti vaco sutvā      udaggo bāvarī ahu    
sokassa tanuko āsi        pītiñca vipulaṃ labhi .

So bāvarī attamano udaggo    
taṃ devataṃ pucchati vedajāto    
katamamhi [^526-1] gāme nigamamhi vā pana    
katamamhi vā janapade lokanātho    
yattha gantvā namassemu    
sambuddhaṃ dipaduttamaṃ .

Sāvatthiyaṃ kosalamandire jino    
pahūtapañño varabhūrimedhaso    
so sakyaputto vidhuro anāsavo    
muddhādhipātassa vidū narāsabho .

Tato āmantayi sisse       brāhmaṇe mantapārage [^526-2]    
etha māṇavā akkhissaṃ       suṇotha vacanaṃ mama    
yasseso dullabho loke     pātubhāvo abhiṇhaso    
svājja lokamhi uppanno    sambuddho iti vissuto    
khippaṃ gantvāna sāvatthiṃ      passavho dipaduttamaṃ .

Kathaṃ carahi jānemu          disvā buddhoti brāhmaṇa    
ajānataṃ no pabrūhi         yathā jānemu taṃ mayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 527 -->
<a id="page-527"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 527]*

Āgatāni hi mantesu        mahāpurisalakkhaṇā    
dvattiṃsā ca byakkhyātā [^527-1]  samattā anupubbaso .

Yassete honti gattesu     mahāpurisalakkhaṇā    
duveva tassa [^527-2] gatiyo      tatiyā hi na vijjati    
sace agāraṃ āvasati [^527-3]      vijeyya paṭhaviṃ imaṃ    
adaṇḍena asatthena         dhammena manusāsati    
sace ca so pabbajati        agārā anagāriyaṃ    
vivaṭacchado sambuddho        arahā bhavati anuttaro .

Jātigottañca lakkhaṇaṃ        mante sisse punāpare    
muddhaṃ muddhādhipātañca        manasā yeva pucchatha .

Anāvaraṇadassāvī           yadi buddho bhavissati    
manasā pucchite pañhe       vācāya visajjessati .(    
bāvarissa vaco sutvā       sissā soḷasa brāhmaṇā    
ajito tissametteyyo      puṇṇako atha mettagū    
dhotako upasīvo ca         nando ca atha hemako    
todeyyakappā dubhayo       jatukaṇṇī ca paṇḍito    
bhadrāvudho udayo ca        posālo cāpi brāhmaṇo    
mogharājā ca medhāvī       piṅgiyo ca mahāisi    
paccekagaṇino sabbe        sabbalokassa vissutā    
jhāyī jhānaratā dhīrā        pubbavāsanavāsitā    
#[^527-1] po. Byākhātā . Yu. Vyākhyātā . [^527-2] po. Dveyevassa .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 528 -->
<a id="page-528"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 528]*

Bāvariṃ abhivādetvā        katvā ca naṃ padakkhiṇaṃ    
jaṭājinadharā sabbe         pakkāmama uttarāmukhā    
muḷakassa [^528-1] patiṭṭhānaṃ       puraṃ [^528-2] māhissatiṃ tadā    
ujjeniṃ cāpi gonaddhaṃ       vedisaṃ vanasavhayaṃ    
kosambiṃ vāpi sāketaṃ       sāvatthiñca puruttamaṃ    
setabyaṃ kapilavatthuṃ          kusinārañca mandiraṃ    
pāvañca bhoganagaraṃ          vesāliṃ māgadhaṃ puraṃ    
pāsāṇakaṃ cetiyañca         ramaṇīyaṃ manoramaṃ    
tasitova udakaṃ [^528-3]  sītaṃ       mahālābhaṃva vāṇijo    
chāyaṃ ghammābhitatto ca       turitā pabbatamāruhuṃ .

Bhagavā ca tamhi samaye       bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhato    
bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ deseti        sīhova nadatī vane .

Ajito addasa sambuddhaṃ       vītaraṃsiṃva bhāṇumaṃ    
candaṃ yathā paṇṇarase        pāripūriṃ upāgataṃ .

Athassa gatte disvāna       paripūraṃ [^528-4] viyañjanaṃ    
ekamantaṃ ṭhito haṭṭho       manopañhe apucchatha    
ādissa jammanaṃ brūhi        gottaṃ brūhi salakkhaṇaṃ    
mantesu pāramiṃ brūhi        katī vāceti brāhmaṇo .

Vīsaṃ vassasataṃ āyu          so ca gottena bāvarī    
tīṇissa [^528-5] lakkhaṇā gatte    tiṇṇaṃ vedānapāragū    
#[^528-1] yu. Aḷakassa . [^528-2] yu. Purimaṃ ... . [^528-3] po. Yu. Tasitovudakaṃ . [^528-4] yu. Puripūrañca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 529 -->
<a id="page-529"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 529]*

Lakkhaṇe iti hāse ca       sanighaṇḍusakeṭubhe    
pañca satāni vāceti        saddhamme pāramiṃ gato .

Lakkhaṇānaṃ pavicayaṃ           bāvarissa naruttama    
taṇhacchida [^529-1] pakāsehi      mā no kaṅkhāyitaṃ ahu .

Mukhaṃ jivhāya chādeti        uṇṇāssa bhamukantare    
kosohitaṃ vatthaguyhaṃ         evaṃ jānāhi māṇava .

Pucchaṃ hi kañci [^529-2] asuṇanto   sutvā pañhe byākate    
vicinteti jano sabbo       vedajāto katañjalī    
ko nu devo vā brahmā    vā indo vāpi sujampati    
manasā pucchite pañhe       kametaṃ paṭibhāsati .

Muddhaṃ muddhādhipātañca        bāvarī paripucchati    
taṃ byākarohi bhagavā        kaṅkhaṃ vinaya no ise .

Avijjā muddhāti vijānāhi [^529-3]  vijjā muddhādhipātanī    
saddhāsatisamādhīhi           chandaviriyena saṃyutā .

Tato vedena mahatā        saṇṭhambhetvāna māṇavo    
ekaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā         pādesu sirasā pati    
bāvarī brāhmaṇo bhoto     saha sissehi mārisa    
udaggacitto sumano         pāde vandati cakkhuma .

Sukhito bāvarī hotu         saha sissehi brāhmaṇo    
tvaṃ vāpi [^529-4] sukhito hohi    ciraṃ jīvāhi māṇava .

#[^529-1] po. Taṃ pucchaṃ . Ma. Kaṅkhacchida . [^529-2] po. Ma. Yu. Pucchañhi kiñci .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 530 -->
<a id="page-530"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 530]*

Bāvarissa ca tuyhaṃ vā       sabbesaṃ sabbasaṃsayaṃ    
katāvakāsā pucchavho       yaṅkiñci manasicchatha    
sambuddhena katokāso       nisīditvāna pañjalī    
ajito paṭhamaṃ pañhaṃ          tattha pucchi [^530-1] tathāgatanti .

Vatthugāthā niṭṭhitā .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa paṭhamā ajitapañhā    
[425] [^530-1] kenassu nivuto loko       (iccāyasmā ajito)    
kenassu nappakāsati    
kissābhilepanaṃ brūsi         kiṃsu tassa mahabbhayaṃ .

Avijjāya nivuto loko      (ajitāti bhagavā)    
vevicchā (pamādā) nappakāsati    
jappābhilepanaṃ brūmi         dukkhamassa mahabbhayaṃ .

Savanti sabbadhi sotā        (iccāyasmā ajito)    
sotānaṃ kiṃ nivāraṇaṃ    
sotānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūhi         kena sotā pithiyyare [^530-2] .

Yāni sotāni lokasmiṃ       (ajitāti bhagavā) sati tesaṃ nivāraṇaṃ    
sotānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūmi         paññāyete pithiyyare [^530-3]  .

Paññā ceva satī ca [^530-4]      (iccāyasmā ajito) nāmarūpañca mārisa    
#[^530-1] ma. Yu. .. Pucchā .  Ito paraṃ sabbattha īdisameva .  2-[^530-3] ma. Pidhiyyare .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 531 -->
<a id="page-531"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 531]*

Etamme puṭṭho pabrūmi      katthetaṃ uparujjhati .

Yametaṃ pañhaṃ apucchi         ajita taṃ vadāmi te    
yattha nāmañca rūpañca        asesaṃ uparujjhati    
viññāṇassa nirodhena        etthetaṃ uparujjhati .

Ye ca saṅkhātadhammāse      ye ca sekhā puthū idha    
tesaṃ me nipako iriyaṃ       puṭṭho pabrūhi mārisa .

Kāmesu nābhigijjheyya       manasānāvilo siyā    
kusalo sabbadhammānaṃ         sato bhikkhu paribbajeti .

Ajitamāṇavakapañhā paṭhamā .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa    
dutiyā tissametteyyapañhā    
[426] [^531-2] kodha santusito loke       (iccāyasmā tissametteyyo 1-)    
kassa no santi iñjitā    
ko ubhantamabhiññāya         majjhe mantā na limpati    
kaṃ brūsi mahāpurisoti        ko idha sibbanimaccagā [^531-2] .

Kāmesu brahmacariyavā       (metteyyāti bhagavā)  
vītataṇho sadā sato  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 532 -->
<a id="page-532"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 532]*

Saṅkhāya nibbuto bhikkhu       tassa no santi iñjitā    
so ubhantamabhiññāya         majjhe mantā na limpati    
taṃ brūmi mahāpurisoti        so idha sibbanimaccagāti .

Tissametteyyamāṇavakapañhā dutiyā .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa tatiyā puṇṇakapañhā    
[427] [^532-3] anejaṃ mūladassāviṃ (iccāyasmā puṇṇako)    
atthī pañhena āgamaṃ    
kiṃ nissitā isayo manujā    
khattiyā brāhmaṇā devatānaṃ    
yaññamakappiṃsu [^532-1] puthūdha [^532-2] loke    
pucchāmi taṃ bhagavā brūhi me taṃ .

Ye kecime isayo manujā (puṇṇakāti bhagavā)    
khattiyā brāhmaṇā devatānaṃ    
yaññamakappiṃsu [^532-1] puthūdha [^532-2] loke    
āsiṃsamānā puṇṇaka itthataṃ [^532-3]    
jaraṃ [^532-4] sitā yaññamakappayiṃsu .

Ye kecime isayo manujā (iccāyasmā puṇṇako)    
khattiyā brāhmaṇā devatānaṃ    
#[^532-1] po. Yu. Sabbattha yaññamakappayiṃsu .  [^532-2] yu. Pudū idha .  [^532-3] ma. Itthattaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 533 -->
<a id="page-533"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 533]*

Kaññamakappiṃsu puthūdha loke    
kaccissu te bhagavā (yaññapathe) appamattā    
atāru jātiñca jarañca mārisa    
pucchāmi taṃ bhagavā brūhi me taṃ .

Āsiṃsanti [^533-1] thomayanti (abhijappanti) juhanti (puṇṇakāti bhagavā)    
kāmābhijappanti paṭicca lābhaṃ    
te yājayogā bhavarāgarattā    
nātariṃsu jātijaranti brūmi .

Te ce [^533-2] nātariṃsu yājayogā (iccāyasmā puṇṇako)    
yaññehi jātiñca jarañca mārisa    
atha ko carahi devamanussaloke    
atāri jātiñca jarañca mārisa    
pucchāmi taṃ bhagavā brūhi me taṃ .

Saṅkhāya lokasmiṃ parovarāni [^533-3]  (puṇṇakāti bhagavā)    
yassiñjitaṃ natthi kuhiñci loke    
santo vidhūmo anigho nirāso    
atāri so jātijarañca brūmīti .

Puṇṇakamāṇavakapañhā tatiyā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 534 -->
<a id="page-534"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 534]*

Suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa catutthī mettagūpañhā    
[428] [^534-4] pucchāmi taṃ bhagavā brūhi me taṃ (iccāyasmā mettagū)    
maññāmi taṃ vedaguṃ bhāvitattaṃ    
kuto nu dukkhā samupāgatāme [^534-1]    
ye keci lokasmiṃ anekarūpā .

Dukkhassa ve [^534-2] maṃ pabhavaṃ apucchasi (mettagūti bhagavā)    
tante pavakkhāmi yathā pajānaṃ    
upadhīnidānā pabhavanti dukkhā    
ye keci lokasmiṃ anekarūpā    
yo ve avidvā [^534-3]  upadhiṃ karoti    
punappunaṃ dukkhamupeti mando    
tasmā pajānaṃ [^534-4] upadhiṃ na kayirā    
dukkhassa jātippabhavānupassī .

Yantaṃ apucchimha akittayi [^534-5] no    
aññaṃ [^534-6] pucchāma [^534-7] tadiṅgha brūhi    
kathaṃ nu dhīrā vitaranti oghaṃ    
jātijaraṃ [^534-8] sokapariddavañca    
tamme munī sādhu viyākarohi    
tathā hi te vidito esa dhammo .

#[^534-1] ma. Yu. Samudāgaṇā ime . [^534-2] po. Ce . [^534-3] po. Aviddhā . [^534-4] yu. Tasmā    
#hi jānaṃ . [^534-5] ma. Yu. Akittayī . [^534-6] ma. Yu. Aññaṃ taṃ . [^534-7] yu. Pucchāmi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 535 -->
<a id="page-535"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 535]*

kittayissāmi te dhammaṃ (mettagūti bhagavā) diṭṭhe dhamme anītihaṃ    
yaṃ viditvā sato caraṃ        tare loke visattikaṃ .

Tañcāhaṃ abhinandāmi         mahesi dhammamuttamaṃ    
yaṃ viditvā sato caraṃ        tare loke visattikaṃ .

Yaṅkiñci sampajānāsi (mettagūti bhagavā)    
uddhaṃ adho tiriyañcāpi majjhe    
etesu nandiñca nivesanañca    
panujja viññāṇaṃ bhave na tiṭṭhe    
evaṃvihārī sato appamatto    
bhikkhu caraṃ hitvā mamāyitāni    
jātijaraṃ sokapariddavañca    
idheva vidvā pajaheyya dukkhaṃ .

Etābhinandāmi vaco mahesino    
sukittitaṃ gotama nūpadhīkaṃ    
addhā hi bhagavā pahāsi dukkhaṃ    
tathā hi te vidito esa dhammo .

Te cāpi nūna pajaheyyu dukkhaṃ    
ye tvaṃ munī aṭṭhitaṃ ovadeyya    
taṃ taṃ namassāmi samecca nāga    
appeva maṃ (bhagavā) aṭṭhitaṃ ovadeyya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 536 -->
<a id="page-536"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 536]*

Yaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vedaguṃ abhijaññaṃ [^536-1]    
akiñcanaṃ kāmabhave asattaṃ    
addhā hi so oghamimaṃ atāri    
tiṇṇo ca pāraṃ akhilo akaṅkho    
vidvā ca so [^536-2] vedagū naro idha    
bhavābhave saṅgamimaṃ visajja    
so vītataṇho anigho nirāso    
atāri so jātijaranti brūmīti .

Mettagūmāṇavakapañhā catutthī .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa pañcamī dhotakapañhā    
[429] [^536-5] pucchāmi taṃ bhagavā          brūhimetaṃ (iccāyasmā dhotako)    
vācābhikaṅkhāmi mahesi tuyhaṃ    
tava sutvāna nigghosaṃ        sikkhe nibbānamattano .

Tena hātappaṃ karohi        (dhotakāti bhagavā) idhevanipakosato    
ito sutvāna nigghosaṃ       sikkhe nibbānamattano .

Passāmahaṃ devamanussaloke    
akiñcanaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ iriyamānaṃ    
taṃ taṃ namassāmi samantacakkhu    
#[^536-1] ma. Vedagumābhijaññā . Yu. Vedaguṃ ābhijaññā . [^536-2] po. Vidvā va so . Ma.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 537 -->
<a id="page-537"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 537]*

Pamuñca maṃ sakka kathaṅkathāhi .

Nāhaṃ samissāmi [^537-1] pamocanāya    
kathaṅkathiṃ dhotaka kañci loke    
dhammañca seṭṭhaṃ ājānamāno [^537-2]    
evaṃ tvaṃ oghamimaṃ taresi .

Anusāsa brahme karuṇāyamāno    
vivekadhammaṃ yamahaṃ vijaññaṃ    
yathāhaṃ ākāso ca [^537-3]  abyāpajjamāno    
idheva santo asito careyyaṃ .

Kittayissāmi te santiṃ (dhotakāti bhagavā) diṭṭhe dhamme anītihaṃ    
yaṃ viditvā sato caraṃ        tare loke visattikaṃ .

Tañcāhaṃ abhinandāmi         mahesi santimuttamaṃ    
yaṃ viditvā sato caraṃ        tare loke visattikaṃ .

Yaṅkiñci sampajānāsi (dhotakāti bhagavā)    
uddhaṃ adho tiriyañcāpi majjhe    
evaṃ [^537-4] viditvā saṅgoti loke    
bhavābhavāya mākāsi taṇhanti .

Dhotakamāṇavakapañhā pañcamī .

#[^537-1] ma. Sahissāmi . Yu. Gamissāmi . [^537-2] ma. Abhijānamāno . [^537-3] po. Ma. Va .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 538 -->
<a id="page-538"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 538]*

Suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa chaṭṭhī upasīvapañhā    
[430] [^538-6] eko ahaṃ sakka mahantamoghaṃ (iccāyasmā upasīvo)    
anissito no visahāmi tārituṃ    
ārammaṇaṃ brūhi samantacakkhu    
yaṃ nissito oghamimaṃ tareyya .

Ākiñcaññaṃ pekkhamā no satimā (upasīvāti bhagavā)    
natthīti nissāya tarassu oghaṃ    
kāme pahāya virato kathāhi    
taṇhakkhayaṃ rattamahābhipassa .

Sabbesu kāmesu yo vītarāgo (iccāyasmā upasīvo)    
ākiñcaññaṃ nissito hitvamaññaṃ [^538-1]    
saññāvimokkhe parame vimutto [^538-2]    
tiṭṭhe nu so tattha anānuvāyī [^538-3]  .

Sabbesu kāmesu yo vītarāgo (upasīvāti bhagavā)  
ākiñcaññaṃ nissito hitvamaññaṃ [^538-1]  
saññāvimokkhe parame vimutto [^538-2]  
tiṭṭheyya so tattha anānuvāyī [^538-3]  
tiṭṭhe ce so tattha anānuvāyī 4  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 539 -->
<a id="page-539"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 539]*

Pūgampi vassānaṃ samantacakkhu    
tattheva so sīti siyā vimutto    
bhavetha [^539-1] viññāṇaṃ tathāvidhassa .

Acci yathā vātavegena khittaṃ [^539-2] (upasīvāti bhagavā)  

atthaṃ paleti na upeti saṅkhaṃ    
evaṃ munī nāmakāyā vimutto    
atthaṃ paleti na upeti saṅkhaṃ .

Atthaṅgato so uda vā so natthi [^539-3]    
udāhu ve sassatiyā arogo    
tamme munī sādhu viyākarohi    
tathā hi te vidito esa dhammo .

Atthaṅgatassa na pamāṇamatthi (upasīvāti bhagavā)    
yena naṃ vajju [^539-4] taṃ tassa natthi    
sabbesu dhammesu samūhatesu    
samūhatā vādapathāpi sabbeti .

Upasīvamāṇavakapañhā chaṭṭhī .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa sattamā nandapañhā .

[431] [^539-7] santi loke munayo (iccāyasmā nando)    
janā vadanti tayidaṃ kathaṃ su    
#[^539-1] po. Ma. Cavetha . [^539-2] po. Ma. Khittā . Yu. Khitto . [^539-3] po. So na pahanamatthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 540 -->
<a id="page-540"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 540]*

Ñāṇūpapannaṃ muni no [^540-1] vadanti    
udāhu ve jīvitenūpapannaṃ .

Na diṭṭhiyā na sutiyā na ñāṇena (na sīlabbatena)    
munīdha nanda kusalā vadanti    
visenikatvā anighā nirāsā    
vadanti [^540-2] ye te munayoti brūmi .

Ye kecime samaṇabrāhmaṇā se (iccāyasmā nando)    
diṭṭhe [^540-3]  sutenāpi vadanti suddhiṃ    
sīlabbatenāpi vadanti suddhiṃ    
anekarūpena vadanti suddhiṃ .

Kaccissu te (bhagavā) tattha yathā carantā    
atāru jātiñca jarañca mārisa    
pucchāmi taṃ bhagavā brūhi me taṃ .

Ye kecime samaṇabrāhmaṇā se (nandāti bhagavā)    
diṭṭhe [^540-3]  sutenāpi vadanti suddhiṃ    
sīlabbatenāpi vadanti suddhiṃ    
anekarūpena vadanti suddhiṃ    
kiñcāpi te tattha yathā caranti [^540-4]    
nātariṃsu jātijaranti brūmi .

#[^540-1] ma. Yu. No muniṃ vadanti . [^540-2] ma. Yu. Caranti . [^540-3] ma. Diṭṭhassu tenāpi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 541 -->
<a id="page-541"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 541]*

Ye kecime samaṇabrāhmaṇā se (iccāyasmā nando)    
diṭṭhe sutenāpi vadanti suddhiṃ    
sīlabbatenāpi vadanti suddhiṃ    
anekarūpena vadanti suddhiṃ    
te ce [^541-1] muni brūsi anoghatiṇṇe    
atha ko carahi devamanussaloke    
atāri jātiñca jarañca mārisa    
pucchāmi taṃ bhagavā brūhi me taṃ .

Nāhaṃ sabbe samaṇabrāhmaṇā se (nandāti bhagavā)    
jātijarāya nivutāti brūmi    
yesīdha diṭṭhaṃ va sutaṃ mutaṃ vā    
sīlabbataṃ vāpi pahāya sabbaṃ    
anekarūpampi pahāya sabbaṃ    
taṇhaṃ pariññāya anāsavā ye [^541-2]    
te ve narā oghatiṇṇāti brūmi .

Etābhinandāmi vaco mahesino  
sukittitaṃ gotama nūpadhīkaṃ  
yesīdha diṭṭhaṃ va sutaṃ mutaṃ vā  
sīlabbataṃ vāpi pahāya sabbaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 542 -->
<a id="page-542"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 542]*

Anekarūpampi pahāya sabbaṃ    
taṇhaṃ pariññāya anāsavā ye    
ahampi te oghatiṇṇāti brūmīti .

Nandamāṇavakapañhā sattamā .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa aṭṭhamā hemakapañhā    
[432] [^542-8] yeme pubbe viyākaṃsu       (iccāyasmā hemako)    
(huraṃ gotamasāsanā)    
iccāsi iti bhavissati        sabbantaṃ itihītihaṃ    
sabbantaṃ takkavaḍḍhanaṃ    
(nāhaṃ tattha abhiramiṃ)    
tvañca me dhammamakkhāhi      taṇhānigghātanaṃ muni    
yaṃ viditvā sato caraṃ        tare loke visattikaṃ .

Idha diṭṭhasutamutaṃ [^542-1] (viññātesu) piyarūpesu hemaka    
chandarāgavinodanaṃ           nibbānapadamaccutaṃ    
etadaññāya ye satā       diṭṭhadhammābhinibbutā    
upasantā ca te satā [^542-2]    tiṇṇā loke visattikanti .

Hemakamāṇavakapañhā aṭṭhamā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 543 -->
<a id="page-543"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 543]*

Suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa navamā todeyyapañhā    
[433] [^543-9] yasmiṃ kāmā na vasanti       (iccāyasmā todeyyo)    
taṇhā yassa na vijjati    
kathaṅkathā ca yo tiṇṇo      vimokkho tassa kīdiso .

Yasmiṃ kāmā na vasanti (todeyyāti bhagavā) taṇhā yassa na vijjati    
kathaṅkathā ca yo tiṇṇo      vimokkho tassa nāparo .

Nirāsaso [^543-1] so uda āsasāno    
paññāṇavāso uda paññakappī    
muniṃ ahaṃ sakka yathā [^543-2] vijaññaṃ    
tamme viyācikkha samantacakkhu .

Nirāsaso [^543-3]  so na ca [4] āsasāno    
paññāṇavāso na ca paññakappī    
evampi todeyya muniṃ vijāna    
akiñcanaṃ kāmabhave asattanti .

Todeyyamāṇavakapañhā navamā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 544 -->
<a id="page-544"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 544]*

Suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa dasamā kappapañhā    
[434] 10 majjhe sarasmiṃ tiṭṭhataṃ        (iccāyasmā kappo)    
oghe jāte mahabbhayeh    
jarāmaccuparetānaṃ          dīpaṃ pabrūhi mārisa    
tvañca me dīpamakkhāhi       yathāyidaṃ nāparaṃ siyā .

Majjhe sarasmiṃ tiṭṭhataṃ  (kappāti bhagavā) oghe jāte mahabbhaye    
jarāmaccuparetānaṃ          dīpaṃ pabrūmi kappa te .

Akiñcanaṃ anādānaṃ          etaṃ dīpaṃ anāparaṃ    
nibbānaṃ iti naṃ brūmi        jarāmaccuparikkhayaṃ .

Etadaññāya ye satā       diṭṭhadhammābhinibbutā    
na te māravasānugā        na te mārassa paṭṭhagūti [^544-1] .

Kappamāṇavakapañhā dasamā .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa  
ekādasamā jatukaṇṇīpañhā  
[435] 1[^544-1] sutvānahaṃ vīra akāmakāmiṃ (iccāyasmā jatukaṇṇī)  
oghātigaṃ puṭṭhumakāmamāgamaṃ  
santīpadaṃ brūhi sahājanetta 2  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 545 -->
<a id="page-545"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 545]*

yathātacchaṃ bhagavā brūhi me taṃ    
bhagavā hi kāme abhibhuyya iriyati    
(ādiccova paṭhaviṃ tejī tejasā)    
parittapaññassa me bhūripañña    
ācikkha dhammaṃ yamahaṃ vijaññaṃ    
jātijarāya idha vippahānaṃ .

Kāmesu vinaya gedhaṃ (jatukaṇṇīti bhagavā) nekkhammaṃ daṭṭhu khemato    
uggahitaṃ nirattaṃ vā       mā te vijjittha kiñcanaṃ    
yaṃ pubbe taṃ visosehi     pacchā te māhu kiñcanaṃ    
majjhe ce no gahessasi   upasanto carissasi    
sabbaso nāmarūpasmiṃ       vītagedhassa brāhmaṇa    
āsavāssa na vijjanti     yehi maccuvasaṃ vajeti .

Jatukaṇṇīmāṇavakapañhā ekādasamā .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa  
dvādasamā bhadrāvudhapañhā  
[436] 1[^545-2] okañjahaṃ taṇhacchidaṃ anejaṃ (iccāyasmā bhadrāvudho)  
nandiñjahaṃ oghatiṇṇaṃ vimuttaṃ  
kappañjahaṃ abhiyāce sumedhaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 546 -->
<a id="page-546"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 546]*

Sutvāna nāgassa apanamissanti [^546-1] ito    
nānājanā janapadehi saṅgatā    
tava vīra vākyaṃ abhikaṅkhamānā    
tesaṃ tuvaṃ sādhu viyākarohi    
tathā hi te vidito esa dhammo .

Ādānataṇhaṃ vinayetha sabbaṃ (bhadrāvudhāti bhagavā)    
uddhaṃ adho tiriyañcāpi majjhe    
yaṃ yañhi lokasmiṃ upādiyanti[^546-7]    
teneva māro anveti jantuṃ    
tasmā pajānaṃ na upādiyetha    
bhikkhu sato kiñcanaṃ sabbaloke    
ādānasatte iti pekkhamāno    
pajaṃ imaṃ maccudheyyaṃ visattanti .

Bhadrāvudhamāṇavakapañhā dvādasamā .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa terasamā udayapañhā    
[437] 1[^546-3] jhāyiṃ virajamāsinaṃ (iccāyasmā udayo) katakiccaṃ anāsavaṃ    
pāraguṃ sabbadhammānaṃ         atthī pañhena āgamaṃ    
aññaṃ vimokkhaṃ pabrūhi        avijjāya pabhedanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 547 -->
<a id="page-547"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 547]*

Pahānaṃ kāmachandānaṃ         (udayāti bhagavā) domanassāna cūbhayaṃ    
thīnassa ca panudanaṃ [^547-1]        kukkuccānaṃ nivāraṇaṃ    
upekkhāsatisaṃsuddhaṃ          dhammatakkapurejavaṃ    
aññaṃ vimokkhaṃ [^547-2] pabrūmi     avijjāya pabhedanaṃ .

Kiṃsu saññojano loko       kiṃsu tassa vicāraṇā    
kissassa vippahānena        nibbānaṃ iti vuccati .

Nandisaññojano loko       vitakkassa vicāraṇā    
taṇhāya vippahānena        nibbānaṃ iti vuccati .

Kathaṃsatassa carato           viññāṇaṃ uparujjhati    
bhavantaṃ puṭṭhumāgamma         taṃ sujhoma vaco tava .

Ajjhattañca bahiddhā ca       vedanaṃ nābhinandato    
evaṃsatassa carato          viññāṇaṃ uparujjhatīti .

Udayamāṇavakapañhā terasamā .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa cuddasamā posālapañhā  
[438] 1[^547-4] yo atītaṃ ādisati          (iccāyasmā posālo)  
anejo chinnasaṃsayo  
pāragū [^547-3]  sabbadhammānaṃ      atthī pañhena āgamaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 548 -->
<a id="page-548"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 548]*

Vibhūtarūpasaññissa            sabbakāyappahāyino    
ajjhattañca bahiddhā ca       natthi kiñcīti passato    
ñāṇaṃ sakkānupucchāmi        kathaṃ neyyo tathāvidho .

Viññāṇaṭṭhitiyo sabbā       (posālāti bhagavā)    
abhijānaṃ tathāgato    
tiṭṭhantamenaṃ jānāti        vimuttaṃ tapparāyanaṃ    
ākiñcaññāsambhavaṃ [^548-1]       nandisaññojanaṃ iti    
evamevaṃ [^548-2] abhiññāya      tato tattha vipassati    
evaṃ [^548-3]  ñāṇaṃ kathaṃ [^548-4] tassa  brāhmaṇassa vusīmatoti .

Posālamāṇavakapañhā cuddasamā .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa    
paṇṇarasamā mogharājapañhā    
[439] 1[^548-5] dvāhaṃ sakka [^548-5] apucchissaṃ    (iccāyasmā moghārājā)    
na me byākāsi cakkhumā [^548-6]    
yāvatatiyañca devisi         byākarotīti me sutaṃ    
ayaṃ loko paro loko      brahmaloko sadevako    
diṭṭhinte nābhijānāmi       gotamassa yasassino    
#[^548-1] ma. Ākiññasambhavaṃ ñatvā . Yu. --- ñatvā .  [^548-2] po. Ma. Evametaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 549 -->
<a id="page-549"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 549]*

Evaṃ abhikkantadassāviṃ       atthī pañhena āgamaṃ    
kathaṃ lokaṃ avekkhantaṃ        maccurājā na passati .

Suññato lokaṃ avekkhassu     mogharāja sadā sato    
attānudiṭṭhiṃ ūhacca         evaṃ maccutaro siyā    
evaṃ lokaṃ avekkhantaṃ       maccurājā na passatīti .

Mogharājamāṇavakapañhā paṇṇarasamā .

suttanipāte pañcamassa pārāyanavaggassa    
soḷasamā piṅgiyapañhā    
[440] 1[^549-6] jiṇṇohamasmī abalo vivaṇṇo [^549-1] (iccāyasmā piṅgiyo)    
nettā na suddhā savanaṃ na phāsu    
māhampanassaṃ [^549-2] momuho antarāya [^549-3]    
ācikkha dhammaṃ yamahaṃ vijaññaṃ    
jātijarāya idha vippahānaṃ .

Disvāna rūpesu vihaññamāne (piṅgiyāti bhagavā)    
ruppanti rūpesu janā pamattā    
tasmā tuvaṃ piṅgiya appamatto    
jahassu rūpaṃ apunabbhavāya .

Disā catasso vidisā catasso    
#[^549-1] ma. Yu. Vītavaṇṇo . [^549-2] po. Māhannassa . Ma. Yu. Māhaṃnassaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 550 -->
<a id="page-550"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 550]*

Uddhaṃ adho dasa disā imāyo    
na tuyhaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ asutāmutaṃ vā [^550-1]    
atho aviññātaṃ kiñcimatthi [^550-2] loke    
ācikkha dhammaṃ yamahaṃ vijaññaṃ    
jātijarāya idha vippahānaṃ .

Tañhādhipanne manuje pekkhamāno (piṅgiyāti bhagavā)    
santāpajāte jarasāparete    
tasmā tuvaṃ piṅgiya appamatto    
jahassu taṇhaṃ apunabbhavāyāti .

Piṅgiyamāṇavakapañhā soḷasamā .

[441]  idamavoca  bhagavā  magadhesu  viharanto  pāsāṇacetiye  [^550-3]    
Paricārikasoḷasannaṃ   brāhmaṇānaṃ   ajjhiṭṭho   pañhaṃ   [^550-4]   byākāsi  .[^550-3]    
Ekamekassa   cepi   pañhassa   atthamaññāya   dhammamaññāya   dhammānudhammaṃ    
Paṭipajjeyya    gaccheyyeva    jarāmaraṇassa    pāraṃ    gamanīyā    ime    
Dhammāti tasmā imassa dhammapariyāyassa pārāyanantveva adhivacanaṃ .

[442] ajito tissametteyyo      puṇṇako atha mettagū    
dhotako upasīvo ca         nando ca atha hemako    
todeyyakappā dubhayo       jatukaṇṇī ca paṇḍito    
bhadrāvudho udayo ca        posālo cāpi brāhmaṇo    
#[^550-1] po. Ma. Asutaṃ mutaṃ . Yu. Asutaṃ mutaṃ vā .  [^550-2] po. Kiñcinamatthi . Ma. Yu.

#kiñcanamatthi . [^550-3] ma. Yu. Pāsāṇake cetiye . [^550-4] ma. Puṭṭho puṭṭho .|  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 551 -->
<a id="page-551"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 551]*

Mogharājā ca medhāvī       piṅgiyo ca mahā isi    
ete buddhamupāgañchacha [^551-1]     sampannacaraṇaṃ isiṃ    
pucchantā nipuṇe pañhe      buddhaseṭṭhaṃ upāgamama .

Tesaṃ buddho byākāsi       pañhe puṭṭhe [^551-2] yathātathaṃ    
pañhānaṃ veyyākaraṇena      tosesi brāhmaṇe muni .

Te tositā cakkhumatā       buddhenādiccabandhunā    
brahmacariyamacariṃsu           varapaññassa santike .

Ekamekassa pañhassa        yathā buddhena desitaṃ    
tathā yo paṭipajjeyya       gacche pāraṃ apārato [^551-3]    
apārā pāraṃ gaccheyya      bhāvento maggamuttamaṃ .

Maggo so paragamanāya       tasmā parāyanaṃ iti .

[443] pārāyanamanugāyissaṃ         (iccāyasmā piṅgiyo)    
(yathā addakkhi tathā akkhāti) [^551-4] vimalo bhūrimedhaso    
nikkāmo [^551-5] nibbuto nāgo [^551-6]  kissa hetu musā bhaṇe .

Pahīnamalamohassa            mānamakkhappahāyino    
handāhaṃ kittayissāmi        giraṃ vaṇṇūpasañhitaṃ .

Tamonudo buddho samantacakkhu    
lokantagū sabbabhavātivatto    
anāsavo sabbadukkhappahāno [^551-7]    
#[^551-1] po. Buddhapāgacchacha . Ma. Buddhaṃ upāgacchacha . Yu. ... Upāgañchacha . [^551-2] ma. Yu.

#puṭṭho . [^551-3] po. Apārako . [^551-4] ma. Yathāddakkhi tathākkhāsi . [^551-5] po. Nikkamo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 552 -->
<a id="page-552"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 552]*

Saccavhayo brahmupāsito [^552-1] me .

Dijo yathā kubbanakaṃ pahāya    
bahupphalaṃ kānanaṃ āvaseyya    
evampahaṃ appadasse pahāya    
mahodadhiṃ haṃsarivajjhapatto [^552-2] .

Ye me pubbe viyākaṃsu      (huraṃ gotamasāsanā)    
iccāsi iti bhavissati        sabbantaṃ itihītihaṃ    
sabbantaṃ takkavaḍḍhanaṃ    
(nāhaṃ tattha abhiramiṃ) [^552-3]  .

Eko tamanudāsīno         jutimā so pabhaṅkaro    
gotamo bhūripaññāṇo        gotamo bhūrimedhaso    
yo me dhammamadesesi       sandiṭṭhikamakālikaṃ    
taṇhakkhayamanītikaṃ            yassa natthi upamā kvaci .

Kinnu tamhā vippavasasi       muhuttamapi piṅgiya    
gotamā bhūripaññāṇā        gotamā bhūrimedhasā    
yo te dhammamadesesi       sandiṭṭhikamakālikaṃ    
taṇhakkhayamanītikaṃ            yassa natthi upamā kvaci .

Nāhaṃ tamhā vippavasāmi      muhuttamapi brāhmaṇa    
gotamā bhūripaññāṇā        gotamā bhūrimedhasā    
yo me dhammamadesesi       sandiṭṭhikamakālikaṃ    
#[^552-1] ma. Yu. Brahme upāsito . [^552-2] po. Haṃsorivajjhuppatto . Ma. Haṃsoriva ajjhapatto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 553 -->
<a id="page-553"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 553]*

Taṇhakkhayamanītikaṃ            yassa natthi upamā kvaci .

Passāmi naṃ manasā cakkhunāva    
rattindivaṃ brāhmaṇa appamatto    
namassamāno vivasāmi [^553-1] rattiṃ    
teneva maññāmi avippavāsaṃ .

Saddhā ca pīti ca mano sati ca    
nāmenti [^553-2] me gotamasāsanamhā    
yaṃ yaṃ [^553-3]  disaṃ vajati bhūripañño    
sa tena teneva natohamasmi .

Jiṇṇassa me dubbalathāmakassa    
teneva kāyo na paleti tattha    
saṅkappayantāya vajāmi niccaṃ    
mano hi me brāhmaṇa tena yutto .

Paṅke (sayāno) pariphandamāno dīpā dīpaṃ upallaviṃ [^553-4]    
athaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ          oghatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ    
yathā ahu vakkali muttasaddho    
bhadrāvudho āḷavigotamo ca    
evameva tvampi pamuñcassu saddhaṃ    
gamissasi tvaṃ piṅgiya maccudheyyassa pāraṃ .

Esa bhiyyo pasīdāmi         sutvāna munino vaco    
#[^553-1] po. Ma. Yu. Vivasemi . [^553-2] po. Nāmeti . Ma. Yu. Nāpentime . [^553-3] po. Yañca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 554 -->
<a id="page-554"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 554]*

Vivaṭacchado sambuddho        akhilo paṭibhāṇavā    
adhideve abhiññāya         sabbaṃ vedi parovaraṃ [^554-1]    
pañhānantakaro satthā       kaṅkhīnaṃ paṭijānataṃ    
asaṃhiraṃ asaṅkuppaṃ           yassa natthi upamā kvaci .

Addhā gamissāmi na mettha kaṅkhā    
evaṃ maṃ dhārehi adhimuttacittanti .

Pārāyanavaggo pañcamo .

suttanipāte suttuddānaṃ    
[444] urago dhaniyopi ca          khaggavisāṇo kasi    
cundo bhavo punadeva        vasalo ca karaṇīyañca    
hemavato atha yakkho        vijayaṃ suttavaraṃ muni [^554-2] .

Seṭṭho paṭhamakaṭṭhapavaro vaggo    
dvādasasuttadharo suvibhatto    
desito cakkhumatā vimalena    
suyyati vaggavaro uragoti .

Ratanāmagandho hirimaṅgalanāmo  
sūcilomakapilo ca brāhmaṇadhammo  
nāvā kiṃsīlaüṭṭhāno ca  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 555 -->
<a id="page-555"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 555]*

Rāhulo ca punapi vaṅgīso    
sammāparibbājaniyo [^555-1] ca tattha    
dhammikasuttavaro suvibhatto    
cuddasasuttadharo dutiyamhiq    
cūḷakavaggavaroti tamāhu .

Pabbajjaṃ padhānasubhāsitanāmo    
puraḷāso punareva [^555-2] māgho ca    
sabhiyakeṇiyameva sallanāmo    
vāseṭṭhavaro kokālikopi [^555-3]  ca    
nāḷakasuttavaro suvibhatto    
taṃ anupassī tathā punadeva    
dvādasasuttadharo tatiyamhi    
suyyati vaggavaro mahānāmo .

Kāmaguhaṭṭhakaduṭṭhaṭṭhakanāmo    
suddhaṭṭhavaro paramaṭṭhakanāmo    
jarāmettiyasuttavaro suvibhatto    
pasūramāgandiyo ca pūrabhedo [^555-4]    
kalahavivādo ubho viyuhā ca    
tuvaṭakaattadaṇḍasārīputtā ca    
soḷasasuttadharo catutthamhi    
#[^555-1] ma. Sammāparibbājanīyopicettha . [^555-2] ma. Punadeva . [^555-3] ma. Kālikopica .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 556 -->
<a id="page-556"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 556]*

Aṭṭhakavaggavaroti tamāhu .

Magadhe janapade ramaṇīye    
desavare katapuññanivese    
pāsāṇakacetiyavare suvibhatte    
vasī bhagavā gaṇaseṭṭho    
ubhayaṃ vā puṇṇasamāgataṃ yamhi    
dvādasayojaniyā parisāya    
soḷasabrāhmaṇānaṃ kira puṭṭho    
pucchāya soḷasapañhakammiyā    
nippakāsayi dhammamadāsi    
atthapakāsakabyañjanapuṇṇaṃ    
dhammamadesesi parakhemajaniyaṃ    
lokahitāya jino dipadaggo .

Suttavaraṃ bahudhammavicitraṃ    
sabbakilesamocanahetuṃ [^556-1]    
desayi suttavaraṃ dipadaggo .

Byañjanamatthapadasamayuttaṃ    
akkharasaññitaopamaggāḷhaṃ    
lokavicāraṇañāṇapabhaggaṃ    
desayi suttavaraṃ dipadaggo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 557 -->
<a id="page-557"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 557]*

Rāgamale amalaṃ vimalaggaṃ    
dosamale amalaṃ vimalaggaṃ    
mohamale amalaṃ vimalaggaṃ    
lokavicāraṇañāṇapabhaggaṃ    
desayi suttavaraṃ dipadaggo .

Klesamale amalaṃ vimalaggaṃ    
duccaritamale amalaṃ vimalaggaṃ    
lokavicāraṇañāṇapabhaggaṃ    
desayi suttavaraṃ dipadaggo .

Āsavabandhanayogakilesaṃ    
nivaraṇāni ca tīṇi ca malāni    
tassa kilesapamocanahetuṃ    
desayi suttavaraṃ dipadaggo .

Nimmalasabbakilesapanūdanaṃ    
rāgavirāgamanejamasokaṃ    
santapaṇītasududdasadhammaṃ    
desayi suttavaraṃ dipadaggo .

Rāgañca dosañca bhañjitasantaṃ [^557-1]  
yoni ca duggatipañcaviññāṇaṃ  
taṇhātalaratacchedanatāṇapamokkhaṃ 2  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 558 -->
<a id="page-558"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 558]*

Desayi suttavaraṃ dipadaggo .

Gambhīraduddasasaṇhanipuṇaṃ    
paṇḍitavedaniyanipuṇatthaṃ [^558-1]    
lokavicāraṇañāṇapabhaggaṃ    
desayi suttavaraṃ dipadaggo .

Navaṅgakusumadhuvalavebharaṇaṃ [^558-2]    
indriyajjhānavimokkhavibhattaṃ    
aṭṭhaṅgikamaggavaraṃ varayānaṃ    
desayi suttavaraṃ dipadaggo .

Somupamaṃ vimalaṃ parisuddhaṃ    
aṇṇavamupamā ratanasucittaṃ    
pupphasamaṃ ravimūpamatejaṃ    
desayi suttavaraṃ dipadaggo .

Khemasivaṃ sukhasītalasantaṃ    
maccuttāṇaparamatthaṃ    
tassa sunibbutadassanahetuṃ    
desayi suttavaraṃ dipadaggo .

Suttanipāto niṭṭhito .

#[^558-1] ma. -- vedaniyaṃ --- . [^558-2] ma. _____ mālagīveyyaṃ .
